EdgeStation third-party notices
===============================

EdgeStation includes the third-party software listed below. Each component is
under its own license. The license texts and copyright notices that come with
the components follow the lists, numbered, and each component names the
numbers that apply to it. These licenses cover those components only.

This file is generated by `pnpm notices` (scripts/notices/generate.mjs) in the
EdgeStation repository. Do not edit it by hand.
